### Section: Introduction
- **Level**: `intro`

```text
To check Google Classroom assignments for plagiarism, connect your Google Classroom courses to Checkmark Plagiarism—enabling automated, unlimited scanning across 90+ billion web pages, academic archives, and institutional repositories with side-by-side source matching and keystroke writing playback.

While Google Classroom includes a basic "Originality Reports" feature, free tiers are capped at only 5 assignment scans per course, exclude private student-to-student repository matching, and provide zero insight into whether text was typed or pasted. In contrast, Checkmark Plagiarism integrates seamlessly with Google Classroom , providing unlimited scans, multi-database matching, AI detection, and 15-second essay playback replays in a unified dashboard.

Below is a step-by-step tutorial on checking Google Classroom submissions for plagiarism and interpreting similarity reports.

Checkmark Plagiarism powers Google Classroom integrity by pairing plagiarism detection with essay writing playback , AI detection , autograding , and native Google Classroom integration .
```

### Section: The 4 Steps to Check Google Classroom Essays for Plagiarism
- **Level**: `h2`
- **Hash**: `f0a55539fa35bde4cd48fc055db01071c99fc9e5d58a90c6bba912b4b84c1cc1`

```text
Step 1: One-Click Google Classroom Sync

Sign in to Checkmark using Google Single Sign-On (SSO) and select your course to automatically import student rosters and assignments.

Step 2: Automated Multi-Database Scan

Checkmark scans submitted Google Docs across 90+ billion live/archived web pages, scholarly journals, and your school's private student repository.

Step 3: Side-by-Side Source Inspection

Review highlighted matching passages alongside the original source URL, journal DOI, or matching classmate submission in interactive Source Evidence Cards.

Step 4: Sync Grades & Comments Back

Authorize finalized grades, similarity notes, and rubric feedback to sync directly back into the Google Classroom assignment stream with 1 click.
```
